# Syndicate — Work Lanes

> Lanes are exclusive by file path. Two lanes may not claim the same file.
> Max concurrent OPEN lanes: 3 (see `state.md`).
> Managed by `/lane`. Do not hand-edit while a session is running.

> **History lives in `lanes_history.md`.** This file is read at the start of
> every session, so it carries each lane's CURRENT state plus one prior block --
> **plus any block that declares file claims**, which `lane-guard` reads from
> here and nowhere else. 36 superseded blocks (2,667 lines) were moved out
> verbatim on 2026-08-18. Nothing was summarised or deleted: if a lane's earlier
> reasoning matters, it is there under the same slug.

one belongs to a live or uncertain lane above, and the remedy is to MOVE the
block above the `## Archived lanes` marker — which is editing another lane's
block. Left for each owner. The hazard is real but latent: their claims work
today and would be dropped silently by a future archive pass.

**Method note for the next sweep.** `.syndicate/.current-lane.<uuid>` marker
filenames match archived `sessionId`s exactly (6 of 13 did), so a marker whose
id resolves to an ARCHIVED session is hard evidence the lane is orphaned. The
markers for running sessions did NOT match any roster id, so the mapping proves
death, never life — do not invert it.

### ladder-refusal-control-arm — OPEN — opened 2026-10-22 — session 4ab694ed-003e-4dbe-8966-f39ec57c0b31
- **GOAL VERDICT 2026-10-29 18:04Z (1:04 PM CT): "Measure whether the staking policy denies profitable bets: the paper ladder is the book of rows refused for falling below the suggested stake floor and placed flat anyway, so its realised ROI against its OWN admitted EV says whether the refusal boundary is calibrated - reported per ladder_reason and per ev_band with denominators and a confidence interval, and with no verdict claimed where the interval spans zero." -- GOAL: MET** by the scheduled re-read `ladder-refusal-control-arm-reading` (deploys.md 2026-10-29 18:04Z). Union of document + archive keyed on `idempotency_key`: 7,376 ladder rows, **5,689 settled** (power gate >=400 PASSED), all paper, all flat $1, window 10-20..10-29 (10 dates). **ROI -6.45% +/-2.43 (excludes zero, NEGATIVE)** against admitted net EV -0.08%. `below_suggested_floor` n=5,410 -6.80% +/-2.47; `suggested_not_staked` n=279 +0.29% +/-13.14 (spans 0). Bands vs their OWN admitted net EV: m1to0 n=3,427 -7.48% +/-3.08 (net -0.56%); 0to1 n=1,786 -6.15% +/-4.55 (net +0.42%); 1to2 n=393 +2.27% +/-9.03 (net +1.35%, spans 0); ge2 n=83 -11.87% +/-15.46 (net +2.57%, spans 0). **H1 HOLDS -- not falsified, and now affirmatively supported: the refused rows lose money; the floor is not declining profitable bets.** Fee wedge: realised sits ~6.4pt below admitted EV both GROSS and NET, and even at ZERO fee the `fee_is_upper_bound` rows realise -2.98% +/-3.20 gross -- the fee term is not what makes refused rows lose; the refused tail's admitted EV is overstated. The 10-22 line "gross edge is real" is RETRACTED. Ready to close; this automated run did not close it. No re-arm.
- **OWNER SESSION ARCHIVED 2026-10-22 (session `4ab694ed`). NOTHING IS OWED TO IT.** Remaining work is the frame-guarded scheduled task **`ladder-refusal-control-arm-reading`** (`fireAt` **2026-10-29T18:00:00Z**), gated at **>=400 settled** ladder rows and told to UNION `execution_ledger_archive/` — the live document alone shows 0 settled ladder rows and a reader who skips the archive will wrongly conclude the ladder is ungraded.
- **GOAL VERDICT 2026-10-22 15:40Z (10:40 CDT): GOAL NOT MET — the measurement ran TO SPEC and the question it exists to answer is still unanswered at this n.** The reading was produced exactly as the goal demands (per `ladder_reason`, per `ev_band`, denominators, a 95% interval, and no verdict claimed where the interval spans zero) — and it spans zero, so there is no verdict to claim. **n=80 settled, ROI -17.19% +/-22.14**; `below_suggested_floor` n=75 -13.00% +/-23.15; bands `m1to0` n=43 -4.66% +/-32.17 and `0to1` n=28 -25.98% +/-35.30, all spanning zero. The only intervals excluding zero are n=5 and n=8, the two noisiest of eight cells, and are shown but not relied on. **H1 NOT FALSIFIED** — falsification required a POSITIVE ROI whose interval excludes zero. **The one result that needs no significance:** admitted EV is **+2.90% gross against -0.04% NET** across all 80 and in every band — a statement about PREDICTED values, so the floor is refusing rows whose gross edge is real and whose net edge is ~zero, and the refusal boundary is set almost entirely by the FEE term. That reframes the question to "is the fee model right", since Kalshi rows carry `fee_is_upper_bound: true`. **Method, nearly a false finding:** the live document alone shows 0 settled ladder rows and every paper venue at 0% settled (vs live kalshi 93%, polymarket 92%), which is `_trim_to_cap` archiving settled PAPER rows FIRST — any paper reading must UNION `execution_ledger_archive/` (9,166 rows / 8,866 settled; lag median 25.5h). **RE-ARMED** at >=400 settled, additionally split by `fee_is_upper_bound` against `ev_net_pct`, as frame-guarded task `ladder-refusal-control-arm-reading` for 2026-10-29T18:00Z. Detail: `findings_2026-10-22_ladder_refusal_control_arm.md`, todo `#724`.
- Goal: Measure whether the staking policy denies profitable bets: the paper ladder is the book of rows refused for falling below the suggested stake floor and placed flat anyway, so its realised ROI against its OWN admitted EV says whether the refusal boundary is calibrated - reported per ladder_reason and per ev_band with denominators and a confidence interval, and with no verdict claimed where the interval spans zero.

- Hypothesis: PRE-REGISTERED 2026-10-22 15:25Z BEFORE computing any ladder outcome. H1: the refused rows realise at or below break-even once fees are counted - the floor is doing its job. The population is the low-EV tail by construction (ev_band m1to0 538 of 878 and 0to1 288) so it must NOT be compared against the staked book pooled; the self-contained test is realised ROI against each bands OWN admitted ev_pct and ev_net_pct. The ladder is flat 1 dollar per row so ROI needs no stake weighting.
- Falsification test: H1 is FALSIFIED if ladder ROI net of fees is POSITIVE with a confidence interval excluding zero, in which case the suggested floor is declining bets that make money and the floor itself is the defect. NOT FALSIFIED on the 2026-10-22 read: nothing is positive-and-significant and every interval with n>=28 spans zero.
- Verification: From the execution ledger AND its archive together (the live document alone shows 0 settled ladder rows because settled paper rows are trimmed out first): settled ladder rows, flat stake, ROI with its denominator and interval, split by ladder_reason and ev_band, each band against its own admitted EV. ## 2026-08-17 - THE LEDGER IS A RECORD, NOT EVIDENCE (the inverse of the same day's other lesson)

I relayed *"two uncommitted soccer fixes at risk of being lost"* to the
coordinator as an action item. **It came from a lane entry, not from a
measurement.** `git status` was empty and fix #1 was already on main.

**This is the exact inverse of the three errors recorded above it today.** There
I called healthy things BROKEN from a null lookup. Here I called a committed
thing AT RISK from a written claim I never checked. **Same root cause: treating
a statement as a reading.**

`.syndicate/**` records what was true WHEN WRITTEN. This lane was last touched
two days before I quoted it. **Before acting on or forwarding a ledger claim
about the state of the working tree - uncommitted work, missing files, a broken
service - re-measure it.** The cost here was small (a wrong action item, since
retracted). The cost of the reverse - deleting or "rescuing" files on a stale
claim - would not have been.

The pool dies BEFORE any name map: 10-09/10-10 (written 10-09 00:35Z-12:36Z, before P2's native history backfill) team_not_in_history 5/8 + no_recent_history (preseason guard :2326-2344) 3/8; 10-11/10-12 no_recent_history 16/16. #473's `no_espn_id_map`/`no_team_rows` (1d5a521f) are the TEAMMATE-EFFECTS diag (`lineup_effects`, orchestrator/connected_game.py:234/:279), not the pool. Agrees with nba-lineup-pools FINDINGS (5) (this lane = app session ee14e4ec = CLI uuid 7693274b; both ids are the same session).
  - SECOND, REAL GAP (not this goal -- a mechanism, so a lead, not a fix): teammate effects get NO name map pregame on 24/24 sides incl. after 45e03ae2, because connected_game.py:54 is its own port of the map ending `out or _from_pbp_history()` (no native fallback; and NBA's pbp_espn_history exists only as .parquet, which it never reads). Switching it on is a mechanism added to a calibrated engine (standard: re-fit what absorbed it); DAL_HOU's `no_team_rows` also says the lineup_* tables lack teams. Needs its own lane + user decision.
  - H2 MET (REGULAR SEASON WORKS NOW): scripts/replay_nba_pregame_lineup_pools.py = production export_props_predictions_local, roster_mode pregame (production's own: smart_sim context roster_mode=pregame, asof_date=D-1, verified on 10-11 sims), ESPN boxscore STRIPPED at both summary readers, rotation_stints_history truncated < D from the live copy (P3's prepare_scratch deletes it: REMOVE_ALWAYS). 2025-26 regular, 9 dates / 84 games / 168 sides (10-22,10-23,10-24,11-12,12-05,01-14,02-11,03-11,04-08): 119/168 sides APPLIED, 119/119 reached the engine (home/away_lineups non-empty), 68/84 games with a pool. From the 3rd regular slate: 118/140 sides, 67/70 games. Name map 34-43 keys from native player_checks alone (box empty, no pbp_espn_history.csv). Every refusal is a named designed guard: no_recent_history 26 (10-22 0/24, 10-23 2/4 -- season start), rotation_mapping_too_sparse 14 (pool <5 / mapped minutes <0.5), rotation_minutes_conflict_with_current_signals 9. So the preseason-empty state is BY DESIGN and resolves itself: pools ramp in from the 3rd regular slate (10-24 15/24).
  - D-1 WINDOW (for nba-lineup-pools' function, sent to that session): pregame passes asof D-1 (smart_sim.py:1207-1213, :1507) and the window keeps date < cutoff (bpss:2321), so D-1's games are never in a pregame pool. Counterfactual with the slate date: applied flips on 1/168 sides (PHX 04-08), pool content on 48. Small.
  - LATENT CRASH FOUND: bpss:2608 (bad_rotation_total_minutes branch of the per-game-stints path) calls `rotation_sim_minutes_from_history(` -- undefined; NameError reproduced by tests/test_nba_pregame_lineup_pools.py. Non-pregame only. Fix needs a loan (file claimed by nba-lineup-pools; requested 2026-10-12).
